Who Plays Thrain In The Lord Of The Rings: The Rings Of Power Season 3

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

You shall not pass! This article contains minor spoilers for "The Lord of the Rings: The Rings of Power" Season 3, Episodes 1-4.

After two years, "The Rings of Power" is back and as sprawling as ever. In fact, it may be even more so than it already was. Seasons 1 and 2 of the Prime Video series took us all over the map (quite literally, thanks to all those nifty map transitions), from the island kingdom of Númenor far in the West to the volcanic wastes of Mordor in the East. Season 3 expands the borders of Middle-earth even further, taking us to new locations and introducing multiple new characters to keep track of — some of whom seem more trustworthy than others.

Somewhere in the middle of all this is the Dwarf prince known as Thráin, the elder brother of King Durin (Owain Arthur) and a figure referenced with no small amount of suspicion towards the end of Season 2. As viewers quickly find out upon returning to Khazad-dûm, there is a fair bit of internal strife going on within the underground city, and Thráin's arrival only exacerbates the tension in the air. While this is actually an original character created solely for the show, he still requires quite a lot of nuance and complexity to fully bring him to life.

So, who better to do so than character actor Eddie Marsan? Yes, it might be incredibly difficult to tell who's actually playing the stoutly Dwarf underneath that mountain of prosthetics, makeup, and that bushy mane of flaming red hair. But, if you take notice of those piercing eyes and distinctive voice, you just might be able to recognize the talents of a performer you've definitely seen before.

As it turns out, maybe there are some Dwarves that you can only trust as far as you can toss them (with respect to Gimli). While watching Thrain do his mischievous thing throughout the early episodes of "The Rings of Power" this season, you'll likely find yourself wondering what else you've seen Eddie Marsan appear in before. In a quirk of scheduling, his most recent high-profile role was actually in this year's "Clayface," where he portrayed Frank Fitzpatrick. As the shady talent manager to Matt Hagen (Tom Rhys Harries), this is a perfect example of the kind of slippery character Marsan plays so well. Before that, he popped up in three episodes of the Netflix series "Heartstopper" and had supporting roles in three of director Guy Ritchie's most recent movies: "Operation Fortune: Ruse de Guerre," "Wrath of Man," and "The Gentlemen."

You'd have to go back a little further to pinpoint what might be his most well-known role of all, however. He was cast in the 2009 "Sherlock Holmes" film as the famous Inspector Lestrade, a role he would then reprise in the 2011 sequel "A Game of Shadows" (yes, both of which were also directed by Ritchie). Having worked regularly since the 1990s, the English actor can be seen in a prolific amount of titles over the decades. Some notable examples include Martin Scorsese's "Gangs of New York," the Wachowski Sisters' "V for Vendetta," "Mission: Impossible III," "Miami Vice," "The World's End," "Deadpool 2," and far too many more to name.

In short, Marsan is an acting veteran who knew exactly how to breathe life into a newly-arriving figure like Thráin.

New episodes of "The Rings of Power" Season 3 premiere on Prime Video every Wednesday.

GdS: Recall, Read or a return – Milan evaluating solutions for right-back role

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

AC Milan are looking for a new right-sided defender and some solutions have reportedly emerged in view of January.

A report from La Gazzetta dello Sport (seen below) this morning sheds some light on Milan’s transfer strategy ahead of the winter. The priorities are a new central defender and a new striker, with Goncalo Inacio of Sporting CP and Nicolo Tresoldi of Club Brugge the dreams for the respective roles.

Read SempreMilan ad-free and get access to exclusive news. Click here for a free trial!

However, a third signing could arrive too. The serious injuries to Samuel Chukwueze and Alexis Saelemaekers (who will not return until well into February), combined with the shift to a four-man defensive line have forced a change in plans.

The aforementioned events have convinced the Rossoneri’s transfer team of the need for a reinforcement on the right flank. More specifically, they want a player capable of operating both as a full-back and as a wing-back.

The idea of ​​bringing Zachary Athekame back to Milanello is very much on the table. However, the plan requires the green light from Lyon, the club where the Swiss player has made an excellent start to the season on loan.

It is believed that if Milan push hard, an agreement for Athekame’s immediate return could potentially be reached, though matters of protocol and existing club relations must also be considered. Paulo Fonseca’s departure to Sporting CP is a factor at play, too.

Other options under consideration include Manchester United’s Diogo Dalot, who spent the 2020-21 season at Milan and knows Amorim.

Finally, there is the 20-year-old Givairo Read of Feyenoord, who caught the eye at San Siro two years ago while facing Rafael Leão during a Champions League play-off.

GIVĒON Returns to Long Beach for the Ultimate Victory Lap | Billboard Presents

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

In a special Driven by Legacy episode of Billboard Presents, presented by Lexus, eight-time Grammy nominee GIVĒON takes Billboard’s Carl Lamarre on a nostalgic ride through his beloved hometown of Long Beach, California, revisiting the people, places and memories that shaped his journey to stardom.

From bringing a student to tears during a surprise visit to his alma mater, Long Beach Poly High School, to returning to Bubba Gump Shrimp Co., GIVĒON reflects on his humble beginnings. He also stops by Fingerprints Records, the local record store where his own albums now line the shelves — a full-circle moment years in the making.

From teenage dreams of stardom to performing at Madison Square Garden, the hometown hero reveals a more personal side of himself, celebrating the roots that shaped his sound, the community that raised him and the West Coast legacy that continues to drive him forward.

Long Beach has always been a city that writes its own rules when it comes to West Coast hip-hop and R&B. I’m Carl Lamarre, executive editor of R&B/hip-hop at Billboard, and today, in the first ever 2027 Lexus TZ, I’m exploring how building a lasting legacy isn’t just about where you’re headed; it’s about the intentional roots and human connections that have paved the way. GIVĒON has made it through real emotion and songs that people can connect with. He’s taking me through his hometown of Long Beach to show me the culture, places and memories that shaped him and the legacy that continues to drive him forward. GIVĒON, my brother.

Yeah, it’s always a pleasure, man. This looks like a special location. Tell us where we are.

So we are at Long Beach Poly High School, my high school. Class of 2013.

I don’t know. No, I’m just playing. I just wanted to just like a like a victory lap.

Pay homage. Any chance I get to put a spotlight on Long Beach, I want to do that. I want to do it a little more too. So I think it’s going to be special for the kids that go here as well.

I mean, listen, being in these hallways, like what instant memories pop out for you?

I mean, I tried to stay out the office, but sometimes I was just sleeping because I was I would do morning practice as well.

So we’d have to get here before school. Practice, shower, and then go to class.

That’s pretty nuts. I don’t know why the hell I was doing all that. What the hell? Oh my God!

Pokémon TCG 30th Celebration Ultra-Premium Collections: Live restock updates at Amazon, Walmart, and Target (Nov. 12)

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

The Pokémon TCG's 30th Celebration Ultra-Premium Collections have finally arrived, but getting hold of either the Espeon (Day) or Umbreon (Night) edition at a reasonable price has been proving difficult, even with new listings regularly going live across major U.S. trading card retailers.

Released on Nov. 6, these enormous anniversary boxes have become two of the most sought-after Pokémon products of the year — just as much as the 30th Celebration ETB. Along with the many early Pokemon Black Friday deals, we've found several places to buy both versions, with Walmart currently offering the lowest prices among top retailers. However, there are some important differences between the available offers and the official restocks.

We've rounded up the retailers worth checking out, the latest prices, and everything you need to know to secure one of these special collections ahead of Black Friday.

These are the main U.S. retailers to bookmark for the Espeon and Umbreon collections, including their current stock situation:

Amazon: Currently, no stock or listings are available through the retailer directly or via third-party sellers, but we’ve added a go-to search results page so you can quickly check for any restocks (we expect more this week).

Best Buy (Day and Night): Official stock is hard to find, but third-party listings are still available for both versions. Set up deal alerts to catch any new allocations.

Target (Day): Currently sold out via official stock and third-party sellers.

TCGplayer (Day and Night): The dedicated trading card marketplace has listings for both the Espeon and Umbreon editions of 30th Celebration’s Ultra-Premium Collection at around or under market price.

Walmart (Day and Night): Both Day and Night editions are available through third-party sellers, but they're charging well above retail price. Keep an eye out for cheaper listings.

Demand for the 30th Celebration expansion has been exceptionally strong since its September launch, and these Ultra-Premium Collections are arguably the hottest items in the entire anniversary lineup.

Unlike smaller Pokemon products such as Booster Bundles and Elite Trainer Boxes, each UPC contains a staggering 30 booster packs, including one exclusive Classic Collection pack with three etched cards celebrating the TCG's history. Add in two highly collectible promo Pokémon cards — Espeon and Umbreon — alongside special Pikachu ex promo cards and all the premium extras (playmat, card sleeves, deck box, etc.), and it’s no wonder that there hasn’t been enough supply to meet the demand.

There are two versions to choose from: the green-and-blue Day edition featuring Espeon ex, and the purple-themed Night collection starring Umbreon ex.

Both contain 29 standard 30th Celebration booster packs and one special Classic Collection pack, alongside a foil Pikachu ex promo card and a matching Espeon ex or Umbreon ex promo. You'll also get a themed playmat, 65 card sleeves, a deck box, a metal Pokémon coin, six damage-counter dice, and a code card for Pokémon TCG Live.

The accessories and promotional artwork vary between editions, but you’re effectively getting the same level of contents with either box.

With both versions in high demand, there are a few ways to make tracking down an Espeon or Umbreon Ultra-Premium Collection a little easier:

Keep checking the major retailers: Walmart, Target, Best Buy, Amazon, and GameStop are all worth revisiting throughout the week — as new stock has been constantly reappearing over the last couple of weeks. On the other hand, stock can sell out with little warning, so you’ll need to check regularly.

Set up stock alerts where available: Retailer notifications and independent stock trackers can help you spot fresh inventory without repeatedly refreshing every product page. Best Buy, for instance, even lets you set deal alerts for specific listings.

Compare prices across different sellers: Third-party sellers can be a useful way to secure either collection when official stock is unavailable. Just remember to compare their offers against current market prices (available on TCGplayer), as some listings are considerably more expensive than others.

Check whether you're getting Day or Night: Some retailers list the two versions separately, while GameStop uses combined listings with randomized selections. Make sure you're ordering the right product if you're specifically after Espeon or Umbreon.

Don't overlook in-store availability: Online stock shifts incredibly fast, but checking nearby Target, Best Buy, and GameStop locations could give you another chance to grab a box — as many don’t check physical stores, simply assuming they’ll be automatically sold out, particularly if additional inventory arrives ahead of Black Friday.

It's certainly possible that more Ultra-Premium Collections could come in stock for Black Friday, though no allocations have been confirmed for either collection.

With Black Friday arriving on Nov. 27, there's still a little over two weeks for retailers to receive more inventory and, as mentioned, there have been fleeting stock drops across the major Pokemon TCG retailers — even if most of them come via third-party sellers. Keep in mind that due to the demand for the 30th Celebration UPCs, sellers have little incentive to discount listings much below market price (around $360 per Ultra-Premium Collection).

For now, it's worth keeping the retailer pages above close at hand, so you can compare prices for any available stock and decide what’s best for your trading card budget.

Ben Williams is a freelance writer at Mashable, having joined the team in June 2025. With over 10 years experience in gaming, tech, TV, anime, and film, there’s nothing he hasn’t covered. Alongside Ben’s other work at IGN, Radio Times, Eurogamer, UNILAD Tech, and Rock Paper Shotgun, he also has bylines at sites like GamesRadar+, PCGamesN, ScreenSphere, Twinfinite, ScreenRant, GGRecon, and more.

When away from writing, he spends his downtime playing and watching games, films, and shows he loves covering — whether it’s replaying The Last of Us Part 2, catching Pokémon, rewatching Fullmetal Alchemist: Brotherhood, or bingeing Tarantino movies.

Benson Boone Booked For 2027 NHL Discover Winter Classic

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

The outdoor hockey game will take place on New Year's Eve in Salt Lake City, Utah.

Benson Boone has been booked as the headliner for the 2027 Discover NHL Winter Classic. The “Beautiful Things” singer will provide the entertainment during the outdoor game between the Utah Mammoth and Colorado Avalanche at Rice Eccles Stadium in Salt Lake City, Utah on New Year’s Eve.

The regular-season outdoor game will be televised on TNT, HBO Max and truTV in the U.S. and on Sportsnet and TVAS in Canada on Dec. 31 at 6 p.m. ET, according to an announcement from the NHL.

Boone, who is on the Australian leg of his 2026 Wanted Man tour, will perform during the first intermission of the game. “The Winter Classic is always about creating something unforgettable, and this year we’re taking that to another level. We’re turning the stadium into a true winter wonderland, and Benson Boone is the perfect artist to bring it to life,” said NHL president of events and content Steve Mayer in a statement. “He’s an incredible performer with a real sense of showmanship, and we can’t wait to see what he does on this stage to create a memorable experience for the fans in the stadium and everyone tuning in from home.”

Additional information on the Discover NHL Winter Classic will be announced in coming week. The Mammoth joined the league as an expansion team in 2024 and will be hosting their first-ever edition of the Winter Classic, making them the 32nd NHL franchise to participate in the outdoor event.

Boone is in the midst of the Australian leg of his Wanted Man Tour, with the singer slated to play his second night at Rod Laver Arena in Melbourne on Friday (Nov. 13). After another gig in Melbourne, the singer will move on to the final three dates on the outing at Afterpay Arena in Sydney on Nov. 17, 18 and 19.

« Un changement était nécessaire » : Manchester United vire Michael Carrick avec effet immédiat !

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

Manchester United a officialisé ce jeudi 12 novembre 2026, par l'intermédiaire d'un communiqué, le limogeage avec effet immédiat de son entraîneur Michael Carrick. Plongée dans une crise sportive historique, la direction mancunienne a décidé d'agir sans attendre la fin de la semaine pour redresser la situation.

Arrivé au centre d'entraînement de Carrington à 7h50 ce jeudi matin, Michael Carrick n'aura pas passé beaucoup de temps avec ses joueurs. Le technicien de 45 ans a définitivement quitté les installations du club vers 10h18 après avoir été notifié de son renvoi. Une matinée particulièrement expéditive pour l'Anglais. Michael Carrick a en effet été informé en personne de cette décision par le directeur général Omar Berrada et le directeur du football Jason Wilcox. Ce dénouement fait suite directe à un sommet crucial tenu la veille à Monaco entre Sir Jim Ratcliffe et la famille Glazer, réunis par visioconférence pour sceller définitivement le sort de leur manager.

Pour justifier ce choix fort, la direction mancunienne a publié un communiqué sans détour.

Le dernier match de Michael Carrick sur le banc s'est soldé par un match nul spectaculaire (4-4) arraché in extremis contre Aston Villa à Old Trafford dimanche 8 novembre 2026. Un ultime sursaut qui n'a pas suffi à sauver sa place. Manchester United pointe actuellement à une inquiétante 18e place de Premier League en position de relégable, avec un bilan de 8 points et une seule victoire en 10 journées, décrochée fin août face à Ipswich. C'est tout simplement le pire début de saison du club en 40 ans.

Pourtant, le redressement opéré par le technicien avait suscité de grands espoirs quelques mois plus tôt. Nommé sur le banc mancunien en janvier 2026 en succession de Rúben Amorim, Michael Carrick avait hissé l'équipe à la 3e place en remportant 12 de ses 17 derniers matches de la saison 2025-2026. Michael Carrick avait d'ailleurs prolongé son contrat au mois de mai 2026 à la suite de cette qualification en Ligue des champions, avant que la situation ne s'effondre en ce début d'exercice.

Dans son communiqué officiel, le club anglais a également précisé ses intentions pour la suite immédiate de la saison.

D'après les révélations de la presse européenne, Mauricio Pochettino fait figure de grand favori pour s'installer sur le banc et disposerait d'un accord verbal pour succéder à Michael Carrick. L'Argentin, actuellement sélectionneur des États-Unis, n'est toutefois pas encore officiellement nommé. Manchester United doit d'abord s'acquitter d'une clause libératoire estimée à près de 10 millions de dollars, soit environ 9 millions d'euros, afin de libérer le technicien de son contrat américain.

S'il venait à prendre les commandes de l'équipe première, Mauricio Pochettino deviendrait ainsi le 12e entraîneur de Manchester United depuis le départ de Sir Alex Ferguson en 2013. Un chantier colossal attend le futur manager. Le prochain match de Manchester United est programmé le dimanche 22 novembre 2026 à Anfield face à Liverpool pour le compte de la 11e journée de Premier League.

Zcash Price Fate: ZEC Crypto to Bounce or Fall Further From Critical $1,000 Support?

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

Ahmed Barakat is a journalist and copywriter based in Georgia with a growing focus on blockchain technology, DeFi, AI, privacy, digital assets, and fintech innovation.

The CryptoNews editorial team is composed of seasoned writers specializing in cryptocurrency and blockchain technology. Their expertise ensures comprehensive, accurate, and insightful content for...

Zcash price is struggling to recover above $1,000 after a sharp sell-off pushed the privacy crypto to its lowest levels since September. ZEC is hovering near $980, extending a weekly decline of more than 26% with sellers overwhelming the market.

The breakdown puts a major psychological level in view, with traders watching if bulls can reclaim $1,000 before another wave of selling hits. Zcash is also approaching its 0.5 Fibonacci retracement level, an area that could attract buyers looking for a potential relief rally.

Strangely enough, the decline comes despite Grayscale committing $1 million to support the Zcash ecosystem. Its investment campaign and development funding have failed to reverse the selling pressure. A technical bounce will depend heavily on Bitcoin stabilizing after its own recent weakness. If BTC continues falling, ZEC could struggle to find enough demand to establish a lasting bottom.

Zcash price latest decline has erased much of its September rally, when ZEC climbed toward $1,700 before momentum started fading. The coin fell 13% to 15% during the latest major sell-off, briefly touching $960. Its weekly losses have now exceeded 26%, putting it among the weaker performers in the large-cap market.

Grayscale’s announcement initially offered a positive fundamental development. The asset manager committed $1 million to organizations supporting Zcash development, privacy research, and community initiatives.

Not just that, Grayscale has also launched a nationwide Zcash marketing campaign, starting with a front-page advertising wrap and a full-page ad inside The Wall Street Journal promoting its Zcash ETF (ZCSH).

Some are calling it the greatest edition of @WSJ yet. Grab your copy.The Zcash $ZEC takeover. @ZcashInvest https://t.co/LMcEuSHTb0 pic.twitter.com/0ArtKaVY7b

The first $400,000 has already been distributed among four organizations, including the Zcash Foundation, Shielded Labs, Project Tachyon, and Pretty Good Policy for Zcash. Grayscale also intends to reinvest 100% of its Zcash ETF sponsor fees into network development, marketing, and education for up to one year.

Grayscale’s Zcash ETF, trading under the ticker ZCSH, recorded $46.25 million in outflows during October. November withdrawals have added further pressure, with nearly $30 million leaving the fund through November 11.

Derivatives traders have also faced significant losses. ZEC liquidations reached approximately $18 million over 24 hours, with leveraged long positions accounting for around 72% of the total.

Technically, Zcash is approaching an important decision point after losing the $1,000 psychological level. The 0.5 Fibonacci retracement is now attracting attention as a potential area for buyers to defend. This midpoint could decide whether a correction is becoming exhausted or another leg lower is playing out.

A successful defense could give ZEC room for a short-term recovery, particularly if Bitcoin manages to stabilize and restore confidence across the altcoin market. The first challenge is reclaiming $1,000. ZEC needs to turn that former support into resistance-turned-support again before bulls can build a convincing recovery case.

A sustained move above $1,000 could open the way toward $1,100, followed by the $1,200 region. The latter also aligns with an important technical recovery area after the recent breakdown.

But the bearish scenario remains firmly on the table. If ZEC loses its 0.5 Fibonacci support, traders could start looking toward $900 and potentially the $800 region. That would extend the correction and give September’s rally further room to unwind.

The Relative Strength Index is also approaching oversold territory, with recent readings near 34. This shows that selling momentum has become stretched, although it does not guarantee an immediate reversal.

$1,000 remains the level bulls need to reclaim, while the 0.5 Fibonacci retracement could determine whether the latest correction is nearing exhaustion or has another painful chapter ahead.

10-Minute Morning Yoga for Strength and Surrender

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

Gillian E. Boult is a yoga teacher, registered social worker, psychotherapist, and author. Also an avid hiker and explorer, Gillian brings her mat into diverse landscapes, using nature as a co-regulator to soothe the nervous system, release tension, and restore cognitive focus. Here, she shares a flow in front of a waterfall.

Adding yoga to your morning routine is among the best ways to set the tone for your day. While most mornings are jammed with to-dos and getting ready rituals, taking even a few minutes to roll out your mat teaches your body, breath, and mind that presence and peace are the priority.

This 10-minute yoga practice encourages you to channel the energy of a waterfall—powerful and ever-shifting—beginning your day with strength and surrender. A series of side and forward bends, core-engaging shapes, and lower body stretches wakes up your body while reminding you of its inherent vitality.

No props are needed for this practice—just your body, your breath, and (if available) a jaw-dropping natural backdrop. If you don’t have access to nature, close your eyes when you flow allows and picture yourself in the outdoor space of your dreams, complete with sound.

Begin in a comfortable seated position, such as Easy Pose, rooting down through your sit bones. Rest your hands in your lap and either close your eyes or soften your gaze.

On your next inhalation, sweep both arms overhead and reach tall. Exhale as you take a side bend toward the left, reaching your right arm overhead as you release your left fingertips, hand, or forearm to the ground.

Inhale to come back through center. Extend your right leg out long and center your chest and hips so you’re facing your right foot. With your exhalation, hinge forward in Head-to-Knee Forward Bend, reaching any amount and resting your hands on your calf, ankle, or foot. Feel free to bend your right knee if that’s more comfortable for you.

Inhale to lift your torso, then plant your left hand behind your left hip, pressing into that hand and engaging your hips to lift in a kneeling variation of Wild Thing.

Exhale to release down to your left side body. Rest on your forearm, stack your knees, and use your right hand in front of you for support as you extend your right leg toward the sky. You might want to grab hold of your right foot or leg in this variation of Reclining Hand-to-Big-Toe.

Release your foot, slowly lowering it all the way to the ground and press through its outer edge. Inhale as you press into your left forearm and lift yourself off the mat while reaching your right arm toward the sky in a variation of Side Plank. Option to bend your bottom knee as you bring your foot behind your or to reach your top arm alongside your head.

When you’re ready, lower down to Easy Pose. Repeat the following poses on the opposite side:

With your exhalation lower down and come to a seat. Extend your legs out wide. Grow tall with your inhalation and exhale into Wide-Angled Seated Forward Bend, hinging at your hips and walking your hands forward any amount that is comfortable.

When you’re ready, walk your hands back in and carefully come onto all fours in a Tabletop position.

Once you’re settled in Tabletop, lift your right leg and guide your knee through 3 large hip circles.

Step your right foot through to land between the hands. On an inhalation, reach your arms overhead and sink deeper into your Low Lunge.

With your next exhalation, bring your hands to the mat and tuck your back toes under, straightening your right left and shifting your sit bones back in Half Splits.

When you’re ready, come back to Tabletop and repeat the following poses on the opposite side:

Come back through Tabletop and widen your knees. Touch your toes together and sink your hips back in Child’s Pose. Notice how you feel in your body here, maybe setting an intention for the rest of your day. Let a word come to you.

Stay here for 1-3 minutes or as long as you’d like.

Once you feel complete, press up to kneel in Thunderbolt Pose. Inhale your arms up overhead, and exhale to bring your hands together at heart center.

Thank yourself for taking the time to make it to your mat today.

Latest Canadian budget packs funding for 55,000 new EV chargers

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

Canada’s government wants to make it easier for citizens to drive electric, and they’re putting more than $4 billion in funding for electricity infrastructure and grid modernization that includes more than 55,000 new EV chargers.

The proposed federal budget set aside $4 billion (CAD) for grid improvements, with a $~635 million carveout specifically for EV charging infrastructure with $587 million of that set aside to renew the Zero-Emission Vehicle Infrastructure Program (ZEVIP), which funds the development of new public EV charging stations.

“The charging industry is really encouraged by the multi-year investment in the budget,” explains Travis Allan, president and CEO of the Canadian Charging Infrastructure Council (CCIC) told Electric Autonomy. “We’ll be looking to make sure that we have great programs in place to deal with the barriers faced by people living in condos and apartments, public fast charging, which needs to cover major corridors and metropolitan areas, and then also charging for fleets and medium- and heavy-duty vehicles, which make up a major share of transportation emissions.”

The rest of the $4 billion will be spent on electricity infrastructure expansion over the next 10 years, starting in 2027 and running through 2036. Because it’s Canada where nearly 60% of electrical generation already comes from renewable hydro and wind power, renewable energy is a de-facto priority, with carveouts for the Smart Renewables and Electrification Pathways Program, new data and modeling efforts to guide grid planning, and $1.6 billion to modernize power systems in northern and remote communities.

The charging infrastructure program highlighted here, ZEVIP, would help charge point operators by covering up to 50% of eligible project expenses for most projects, and up to 75% of projects led by indigenous developers with caps of up to $2 million per project.

Canada continues to take steps to separate itself from the wild swings and economic chaos caused by America’s random tariffs, trade policies, and dumb oil wars – and building out an infrastructure that makes it easier for its citizens to give affordable Chinese EVs a chance while strengthening its relationship with the US’ biggest economic rival?

If Washington doesn’t see that for, “Go pound sand, eh?” that it is, then it’s truly lost the plot – but even if the “blue wave” last week was enough to shake a little sense into the folks running the show, it may already be too late to patch things up.

It's still unclear why Alice Huang is in Beijing.

When you purchase through links on our site, we may earn an affiliate commission. Here’s how it works.

Alice Huang, the person allegedly involved in an affair with Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang, has reportedly surfaced in Beijing, marking the first sighting of the Megaspeed executive since the scandal broke out. According to Bloomberg, it’s unclear when the suspected Chinese spy arrived in the capital or why she’s in the area, but she was last spotted leaving Singapore for Shanghai in 2025 after she was detained and questioned during the city-state’s investigation into the $2-billion smuggling case involving Megaspeed.

The Nvidia CEO is now under intense scrutiny after the revelation of his indiscretion with the Megaspeed executive, with Congress threatening to issue a subpoena if he does not respond to their invitation to “a joint, closed-door briefing” with the heads of the House committees on China, Foreign Affairs, and Intelligence. The FBI is also looking into the relationship between the head of the largest AI hardware company in the world and a person suspected of helping sanctioned Chinese companies secure hundreds of thousands of banned H100 and H200 chips. Overnight, a second bombshell report claims that Jensen Huang was also involved in an extramarital affair with Nvidia's VP of health care, Kimberly Powell.

Although the Chinese embassy in Washington has denied any links to Alice Huang, calling it nothing but “groundless smears and slanders” against Beijing, there is speculation that she’s under the control of the Ministry of State Security (MSS), which is the primary agency that handles China’s foreign intelligence and counterintelligence operations. The U.S. intelligence community suggests that she may be passing on information to the MSS, but it remains unclear if she was specifically tasked by Chinese intelligence to target Jensen Huang or if they were only leveraging her relationship with the Nvidia chief to get insights into the U.S. AI industry.

A deeper investigation into Alice Huang, known by her Chinese name as Huang Le, revealed that she supposedly worked in media before jumping into banking and finance. The Wall Street Journal reports that it’s unclear when she met her husband, Kang Wei, but the couple ran a private equity and investment management firm called Shanghai Hexi in 2016. This allowed the couple to invest in companies across industries, including gaming, pharmaceuticals, and, eventually, data centers and cloud computing.

The couple is known for riding trends and isn’t a stranger to scandals. Their firm, Shanghai Hexi, invested in gaming companies in the 2010s, especially when mobile gaming started to boom across the world, while Chinese regulators fined Kang and the company in 2018 for disclosure failures and other violations while it was in the midst of a takeover battle for a corporation. Kang’s moves in the gaming industry are what allowed him to meet Jensen Huang, which eventually led to a close relationship between the couple and the Nvidia chief. Sources told the WSJ that Kang apparently accepted Alice and Jensen’s extra-marital relationship, even sometimes jokingly telling people that she was Jensen’s “personal assistant.”

It seems that the couple is used to leveraging their relationships to gain business advantage, and with investments in AI data centers expected to hit $32 trillion by 2050, Alice Huang’s “close” relationship with Jensen Huang put Shanghai Hexi in an excellent position to take advantage of the trend through Megaspeed and make big money. And with U.S. sanctions making it harder for competitors to acquire Nvidia chips, it appears that their access has turned into a gold mine for the company. But whether Alice Huang acted solely out of business interest with the supposed procurement of Nvidia chips or was leveraged by Chinese authorities to import these chips, the fact remains that $7.5 billion worth of banned chips reportedly made their way into China despite Washington’s efforts.

Official: Crystal Palace Sack Pierre Sage as Manager

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

Crystal Palace have sacked Pierre Sage as their manager after just 16 games in charge, the club have announced.

The Eagles sit bottom of the Premier League after winning just one of their ten games this season, with the team falling to a 3-1 home defeat against Liverpool on Sunday afternoon.

That defeat came on the back of a string of poor results under the Frenchman since he replaced Oliver Glasner in the summer, and the Selhurst Park board have now seen enough to decide that a change is necessary.

In a statement released on their official website, Crystal Palace said:

Crystal Palace Football Club can confirm that Pierre Sage has left his role as Manager.

Three members of Pierre’s coaching staff – Jamal Alioui, Damien Della Santa and Sandy Guichard – have also left the club.

The club would like to place on record its thanks to Pierre and his staff for their efforts during their time in South London, and wish them well for the future.

An update on the process of appointing a new Manager will be provided in due course.

Sage was handed the job on a three-year deal after coaching Lens to a second-place finish in Ligue 1 last season, behind only Paris Saint-Germain, while they also won the Coupe de France.

But at Selhurst Park he has struggled to implement his style, with the 30 goals the team have conceded in all competitions being more than any other team in Europe's top five leagues, alongside Manchester United.

Palace saw Glasner depart after a successful two-and-a-half years at the helm, where he won the FA Cup, Community Shield and Conference League, but Sage saw Maxence Lacroix and Daniel Munoz sold in the summer, while Ismaila Sarr and Jean-Philippe Mateta also pushed for an exit.

The club brought in ten new players to back the manager and help the transition, including Dwight McNeil, Axel Disasi, Quentin Timber, and Anan Khalaili, but they find themselves bottom of the Premier League at the international break, one point adrift of safety.

GIVEMESPORT understands that there are a number of targets under consideration to replace Sage, including Thomas Frank, Sean Dyche, and Vitor Pereira. Tony Popovic has also been discussed, and has been approached in the past, but his lack of Premier League pedigree could go against him.

Crystal Palace need to part ways with Yeremy Pino, as well as Pierre Sage, after Sunday's disastrous loss to Liverpool.

Strategy spent more than twice as much on preferred-share buybacks as Bitcoin. Now comes its MSCI exit

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

The November 30 close creates a financing test after MSTR proceeds funded larger preferred repurchases than Bitcoin purchases during November 2–8.

MSCI has scheduled Strategy’s removal from the MSCI USA Index for the November 30 close, putting a date on a financing test for the Bitcoin treasury company. MSTR common-stock sales recently funded both Bitcoin purchases and a larger preferred-share buyback.

During November 2–8, Strategy allocated $188 million of common-stock proceeds to STRC preferred-share repurchases and $82 million to Bitcoin purchases. The competition for that money makes future MSTR fundraising central to how quickly the company can keep adding Bitcoin.

The November 11 review lists STRATEGY A among the MSCI USA deletions. MSCI’s final-impact announcement applies its non-operating-company methodology in the November review. Existing constituents must fail the ineligibility screen in each of their two most recent annual filing periods; MSCI may also consider the latest interim filing.

Strategy’s November 9 filing reports $284 million in net proceeds from selling 2,051,348 MSTR shares during November 2–8. It reported no sales under the at-the-market offering programs for its listed preferred securities during that period.

A further $14 million of the common-stock proceeds went to USD Cash. Strategy bought 1,036 BTC, bringing its reported holdings to 850,364 BTC as of November 8.

That allocation explains why index membership matters beyond a single stock-market rebalance. Newly issued common shares supplied money for two competing priorities: adding Bitcoin and retiring preferred claims. A reduction in the attractiveness of MSTR issuance could leave less money for either use unless another funding source supplies the difference.

The filing also reported about $18 billion of MSTR stock available for issuance and sale as of November 8. That is remaining offering capacity, not cash already raised or a commitment from investors to buy it.

A tracker selling an existing MSTR share transfers it to another investor. Strategy receives proceeds when it issues new shares. These are separate transactions, so a tracker’s sale does not directly withdraw money from the company or dispose of its Bitcoin.

The potential connection runs through price and demand. If rebalancing weakens either enough to make new MSTR issuance less attractive, Strategy could face a tighter choice between Bitcoin accumulation, preferred buybacks and cash retention. Its June capital framework already makes common-equity issuance dependent on market conditions, capital needs, trading levels and management’s judgment about shareholder value.

The size of that pressure remains uncertain. Strategy’s consultation response estimates that funds linked to MSCI’s global investable indexes held about 13 million shares, or 3.1% of basic shares outstanding. The page supplies no holdings observation date, so those figures do not establish November 12 exposure or the amount trackers will sell.

Strategy has already authorized and used Bitcoin sales. Its October 29 results disclosed approximately $432 million of Bitcoin sold year to date to fund part of its preferred dividends and STRC buybacks. Those sales preceded MSCI’s final deletion decision.

The June monetization program permits sales for reserve funding, dividends and interest, and securities repurchases. It obligates none. Further sales within those purposes depend on management’s capital-allocation judgment; activity beyond authorized purposes or limits requires additional board approval.

Cash also has distinct roles. At November 8, Strategy reported about $5 billion in its USD Reserve supporting preferred dividends and debt interest, alongside $146 million of separate USD Cash for broader treasury uses. Under the June policy, other reserve uses require board authorization. Its July buyback policy specifically bars reserve-funded STRC repurchases and leaves buybacks discretionary.

The next financing disclosures will show whether MSTR or preferred issuance supplies fresh capital, how much goes to Bitcoin and whether buybacks continue to absorb the larger allocation.

Photographer Files $150K Copyright Lawsuit Against Sony Over His Image of Stevie Wonder

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

Photographer Files $150K Copyright Lawsuit Against Sony Over His Image of Stevie Wonder

A photographer has sued Sony, accusing the company of posting his “iconic” 1974 portrait of Stevie Wonder on Instagram without permission.

Longtime photographer Al Satterwhite filed a copyright infringement lawsuit against Sony Music Publishing (SMP) in the U.S. District Court for the Southern District of New York on Sunday, seeking up to $150,000 in damages.

According to a report by Billboard, Satterwhite photographed Wonder sitting at a piano in 1974 and later registered the images with the U.S. Copyright Office. In 2023, SMP allegedly used one of the portraits in an Instagram carousel marking the 50th anniversary of Wonder’s 1973 album Innervisions.

Satterwhite’s lawsuit alleges that SMP posted his “iconic photograph” of Wonder on Instagram without first obtaining a license. He argues that the company’s use of the image cost him a licensing opportunity and potentially affected the market for his work. The complaint also claims that SMP benefited from sharing the photograph with its social media audience without paying him.

In court documents, the photographer claims the alleged infringement by SMP “has harmed, and continues to harm, the potential market for or value of the copyrighted work by robbing Plaintiff of his exclusive right to license the copyrighted work for illustrative and other purposes.”

The photographer is seeking up to $150,000 in statutory damages for copyright infringement, the maximum amount allowed under federal law for the willful infringement of a single work. Satterwhite’s argues that Sony knew, or should have known, that it needed a license to use the image, given its experience handling intellectual property rights in the music industry.

In the complaint, Satterwhite argues that SMP “is a sophisticated media company that understands copyright law and the need to obtain a license to publish the copyrighted works of others.”

According to Billboard, Satterwhite has filed more than a dozen previous lawsuits over the alleged unauthorized use of his photographs of Wonder and other well-known figures, including Arnold Schwarzenegger and Mel Brooks. Satterwhite also filed a lawsuit against Berklee College of Music last year, accusing the institution of using another photograph of Wonder from the same 1974 shoot without permission. He dropped the case voluntarily less than two months later.

Crystal Palace sack Pierre Sage: Club statement and update on ‘advanced talks’ with successor

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

By Oliver Thomas | 12 Nov 2026 12:40, Last updated: 12 Nov 2026 14:19

Crystal Palace have announced that Pierre Sage has been relieved of his duties as head coach.

The 47-year-old has departed Selhurst Park less than five months after he was appointed the successor to Oliver Glasner, who had steered the Eagles to Conference League glory last season and a historic first FA Cup triumph the year before.

Sage arrived at Palace on the back of a successful 2025-26 campaign in charge of Lens, who finished second in Ligue 1 and won their first-ever Coupe de France trophy.

However, the Frenchman was unable to make an immediate impact in South London and has left Palace sitting rock bottom of the Premier League table after losing seven of his opening 10 games (W1 D2).

A 3-1 home defeat to Liverpool last weekend represented a fourth consecutive top-flight defeat for the Eagles that proved to be the final nail in the coffin for Sage, who saw his side concede a division-high 24 goals.

Palace also sit 21st in the 36-team Europa League standings with only one win from their first four games on the board thus far.

Frank de Boer (five) is the only permanent manager in Crystal Palace’s 121-year history who has overseen fewer matches than Sage (16), who departs with a 31.25% win ratio (W5 D3 L8).

Crystal Palace Football Club can confirm that Pierre Sage has left his role as Manager. The club would like to place on record its thanks to Pierre and his staff for their efforts during their time in South London, and wish them well for the future. https://t.co/JcVHA6FIhe https://t.co/VAM8XnpHlq

The Eagles added: "The club thanks Pierre and his staff for their efforts during their time in South London, and wish them well for the future.

"An update on the process of appointing a new manager will be provided in due course."

It is now understood that Sage already has a new managerial job lined up, with a 'verbal agreement' said to be in place.

It was reported at the end of last month that Sean Dyche was the ‘frontrunner’ to replace Sage as Palace head coach.

The likes of Anthony Barry – Thomas Tuchel’s assistant coach at England - Vitor Pereira and Rob Edwards, Jens Berthel Askou and Ole Werner have also been linked with the Eagles job.

However, multiple reports, including one from BBC Sport, claims that Thomas Frank is now in ‘advanced talks’ to take over.

It is thought that Palace wish to finalise Frank’s appointment before their next Premier League game away against Coventry City on November 21.

Frank has been out of work since he was sacked as Tottenham Hotspur boss in February, after less than eight months in charge.

The 53-year-old, who replaced Ange Postecolgou, won only 13 of his 38 games at the helm and left the North London club languishing in 16th place in the Premier League table, five points above the relegation zone.

During a brief, trouble-filled spell in charge, Frank was unable to earn the backing of the Spurs faithful, with his tenure defined by numerous setbacks and awkward blunders including drinking from a coffee cup branded with the Arsenal badge.

However, Frank had enormous success at previous club Brentford during a memorable seven-year spell between 2018 and 2025, guiding the Bees into the Premier League and then establishing them as a competitive top-flight team, reaching a ninth-place finish in 2023.

The Dane won 132 of his 317 games in charge of Brentford and they finished as high as ninth in the Premier League under his tutelage in the 2022-23 season.

Frank stated in June that he had no intentions of taking a new job and opted to conduct media and punditry duties during the 2026 World Cup, but he now appears to be ready to jump back into management and embark on a fresh challenge with Crystal Palace.

Will a Grand Theft Auto game ever be set outside of America? Rockstar offers a small glimmer of hope

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

The Grand Theft Auto games have always been synonymous with America; Americana is baked into the crime series' very being. Across its history, there's only ever been one GTA game set outside America: the London-based expansion of the original game.

GTA 6 follows this template, parodying the American state of Florida and the city of Miami. But what about the next game - will the series always be in America? Not necessarily.

In an interview with The Guardian, Rockstar North co-studio boss Rob Nelson propped the proverbial door open a sliver by saying: "Never say never." He then quickly followed that comment with: "But there’s still so much to do in America".

This is a topic Rockstar has responded to before, most notably when company co-founder and long-time GTA writer Dan Houser - who's no longer at Rockstar - discussed why the series always seemed to return to America. He said it was because cities like Miami and New York and Los Angeles are places where extreme juxtapositions exist. They're places where extreme wealth rubs shoulders with extreme poverty, and where organised crime can be found.

There's also something America infamously has that few other countries do, and that's free availability of guns, which is such a core tenet of a GTA experience.

Nevertheless, Obbe Vermeij - a former technical director at Rockstar North - revealed last year that a GTA: Tokyo almost happened. However, it wasn't going to be made by Rockstar but rather an external team in Japan.

While discussing the subject, Vermeij referred to America as "basically the epicenter of Western culture". "Everybody knows the cities, even people who haven't been there," Vermeij said. "They have a mental image of the cities. I'm afraid we're stuck in this loop of about five American cities. Let's just get used to it."

GTA 6 is just days away from its 19th November release on PS5 and Xbox Series X/S. Rockstar recently clarified the game runs at 30FPS across all platforms and confirmed that while it's available through Xbox Cloud Gaming, it cannot be streamed to PCs or non-Xbox devices.

Mauricio Pochettino should not take Man Utd job - unless two 'needed' signings are made

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

By Oliver Thomas | 12 Nov 2026 12:38

Gary Neville has warned Mauricio Pochettino against becoming the next Manchester United head coach unless he is promised two new signings in January.

It was confirmed on Thursday morning that Michael Carrick has been relieved of his duties just six months after his permanent appointment, with the Red Devils revealing in a statement that they “intend to appoint a permanent successor as quickly as possible.”

The man expected to replace Carrick is former Tottenham Hotspur and Chelsea boss Pochettino, who reportedly has an agreement in principle to swap his current role in charge of the USA national team with the Old Trafford hotseat.

Pochettino, 54, is set to take over a Man United side languishing inside the Premier League relegation zone following the club’s worst start to a season in 40 years.

The struggling Red Devils have looked particularly vulnerable in defence, conceding 19 goals across their opening 10 league games – only Ipswich Town (20) and basement club Crystal Palace (24) having conceded more.

Ahead of Pochettino’s expected arrival, former Man United defender Neville believes that the Argentine should be assured by the club’s hierarchy that he can make at least two defensive signings in January before accepting the manager’s job.

Speaking on The Overlap, Neville said: “If I was Mauricio Pochettino now, I would be literally protecting myself up to the high heaven.

“He'll do that with a pay-off, so do that in your contract, but secondly, he needs two defenders in January, so he's got to insist that he gets two defenders in January.

“He's got to insist that he gets a minimum budget next summer for the transfer window to be able to turn this around. He's got to insist that he gets a budget that follows.

“They have to give it to him or else he shouldn't take the job.”

Neville added: “He's got to insist on the way in that there's some sort of transfer control, some form of budget there that he knows he can meaningfully change the team.

“Mauricio, I don't think is the kind of manager either who would say 'Give me half a billion' - I don't think he's that type of guy, I think he's someone who is a bit more responsible and will look at things.

"He'll want to bring players through and he'll want to develop young players - I get that and he'll do that. I just think he needs to protect himself in these next 12 months with the transfer budget. He has to.

“The only time you can insist upon it is at the very beginning and he needs two defenders in January.”

Neville, who made 602 appearances for Man United and is regarded as one of England’s greatest right-backs, did not hold back on his criticism of the Red Devils’ current defensive options.

“I mean look at the back four that he finished with on Sunday (4-4 draw with Aston Villa). It was [Diogo] Dalot left-back, [Noussair] Mazraoui right-back, Leny Yoro and Ayden Heaven,” Neville added.

“That is probably the worst back four in the Premier League. I don't know a back four that is worse than that in the Premier League right now if I watch it. I can't believe Manchester United have ended up with that back four.”

Man United have been linked with a plethora of defenders throughout this calendar year, but their top left-back target Lewis Hall signed a long-term contract extension at Newcastle in September, seemingly ending their pursuit of the Englishman.

Barcelona’s Alejandro Balde, Manchester City’s Rayan Ait-Nouri, RB Leipzig’s David Raum and Rennes starlet Mahamadou Nagida are among a number of other left-backs who have been linked with Man United.

The likes of Roma’s Wesley, Everton’s Jarrad Branthwaite, Fulham’s Calvin Bassey, Brentford’s Nathan Collins and Nottingham Forest’s Murillo are other defenders who are believed to be on the Red Devils’ radar ahead of the winter window.

Pochettino could be announced as Man United’s new manager before the club travels to Anfield for their next Premier League match against Liverpool on November 22.

Botticelli painting saved from leaving UK goes on view in Oxford

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

The Virgin and Child Enthroned being installed at the Ashmolean Museum

© Andrew Bailey, 2026, courtesy of the Ashmolean Museum, University of Oxford

A painting by Sandro Botticelli at risk of leaving the UK earlier this year has gone on display at the Ashmolean Museum in Oxford where it was first shown in the 1930s.

The Virgin and Child Enthroned (1470s) was bought by the London-based private collection Klesch Collection, which announced plans in May to loan the painting to the Ashmolean for three years. The painting was previously exhibited at the museum in 1934–35 when it was attributed to Botticelli's workshop.

The UK government placed an export bar on the Botticelli painting, valued at £10.2m, in May 2025. The UK Department for Culture, Media and Sport said that the painting had a recommended price of £9,960,000 (plus VAT of £272,000, which can be reclaimed by an eligible institution). The work was sold at Sotheby’s London in late 2024 for £9.7m (with fees).

The painting underwent conservation over the summer to remove discoloured varnish and later retouching, revealing well-preserved details of the Virgin and Child and restoring the original cooler tones, says an Ashmolean statement. The painting is also unusual for its generous use of gold leaf, “although earlier Italian painters regularly applied gold to the backgrounds of their devotional works, it was less widely used by Botticelli's time”, add the statement.

The Botticelli painting was housed in the Convent of San Giuliano in Florence in the early 19th century, and was later transported to a small chapel attached to a group of farmhouses in a nearby village. It then passed into the family of Giovanni Magherini Graziani, who sold it in November 1903 to the Italian dealer, Elia Volpi. Harriet Sarah Jones Loyd (Lady Wantage) bought the work in May 1904 for £5,000. The Botticelli painting had been kept at Betterton House, near Wantage, Berkshire, since 1944.

Founders A. Gary and Dr. Anita Klesch started the Klesch Collection with the acquisition of a set of Four Seasons by Giuseppe Arcimboldo in 2014. The collection of 15th-to-17th-century European art, which also includes works by Caravaggio, Sofonisba Anguissola and Rubens, is regularly loaned to museums and institutions worldwide.

Pierre Sage sacked by Crystal Palace

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

Please refresh the page or navigate to another page on the site to be automatically logged inPlease refresh your browser to be logged in

The Frenchman departs after a fourth consecutive Premier League defeat and with the Eagles bottom of the table

Want to bookmark your favourite articles and stories to read or reference later? Start your Independent Membership today.

Crystal Palace have sacked manager Pierre Sage after the club slumped to bottom of the Premier League table for the first time since December 2017.

The Frenchman moved to Selhurst Park on a three-year contract this summer after an impressive year at Lens, where he led the club to a runner-up finish in Ligue 1, won the Coupe de France and Trophee des Champions titles for the first time, and qualified for the Champions League.

But he was unable to replicate that success across his 16 games in all competitions at Palace, who have conceded the most goals of any top-flight side this season and claimed just seven points out of a possible 30 during his tenure, and sit two points off safety going into the November international break.

A club statement released on Thursday said: “Crystal Palace Football Club can confirm that Pierre Sage has left his role as Manager.

“The club would like to place on record its thanks to Pierre and his staff for their efforts during their time in South London, and wish them well for the future.

“An update on the process of appointing a new Manager will be provided in due course.”

Former Brentford and Tottenham manager Thomas Frank could be considered as Palace now attempt to rescue themselves from a relegation fight, while former Burnley, Everton and Nottingham Forest boss Sean Dyche is also out of work and would be available.

Three members of Sage’s coaching staff, Jamal Alioui, Damien Della Santa and Sandy Guichard, have also departed.

Sage leaves after a 3-1 defeat to Liverpool at Selhurst Park, which marked Palace’s fourth straight league loss and seventh in the top-flight this season. Palace also have one win, two draws and one defeat from four Europa League games and are through to the quarter-finals of the Carabao Cup, where they play Sunderland next month.

The former Lyon boss had replaced perhaps the most successful Crystal Palace manager in modern history in Oliver Glasner, who won the FA Cup and Conference League with the Eagles before moving to Premier League rivals Nottingham Forest at the end of last season.

Secure Solar Futures set to install 313-kW array on Virginia elementary school

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

Secure Solar Futures, a commercial solar contractor, will build a 313-kW solar power system at Preston Park Elementary School in Roanoke, Virginia. Construction of the system is expected to start later this year at no cost to the Roanoke Schools through a 25-year power purchase agreement with Secure Solar Futures.

A total of 531 bifacial solar panels manufactured by VSUN Solar in Houston, Texas, will be installed on the rooftop of the school, and the array is expected to offset 67% of the power used by the school annually. In its first year, the school’s solar system is projected to produce 421 MWh of clean energy, enough to power 44 average Virginia homes.

Under the PPA, the solar power equipment will be provided at no upfront cost to Roanoke Schools. For the 25-year term of the agreement, Pardee Renewable Energy and Secure Solar Futures will own, operate and maintain the solar power system and will sell the energy produced to the school division at a rate lower than the local electric utility.

Preston Park Elementary School is expected to save $581,000 electricity costs over the term of the PPA. After the conclusion of the term, Roanoke Schools can opt to take ownership of the solar power system at a nominal cost, producing its own energy for the remainder of the equipment’s lifespan.

Billy Ludt is managing editor of Solar Power World and currently covers topics on mounting, inverters, installation and operations.

Warrior 2 Teaches You to Balance Effort and Surrender

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

(Photo: Andrew Clark; Design in Canva by Laura Harold)

In Yoga Journal’s Archives series, we share a curated collection of articles originally published in past issues beginning in 1975. This article about how to do Warrior 2 Pose first appeared in the May-June 2000 issue of Yoga Journal. Find more of our Archives here.

Over two millennia ago, one of the most essential teachings of yoga was given on a battlefield, of all places. As recounted in the Bhagavad Gita, Arjuna, the consummate warrior, becomes paralyzed with doubt and fear just as he is about to be called to action. Luckily for him, his chariot driver happens to be none other than the god Krishna, who proceeds to reveal to Arjuna the teachings of yoga to liberate him from his confusion.

In my favorite translation of the Gita, by the late scholar/teacher Eknath Easwaran (Nilgiri Press, 1985), Krishna defines yoga as “wisdom in action”—yogah karmasu kausalam (II.50). He guides Arjuna to reflect upon the source of his actions and find his internal center, where he is free from the fluctuations of the mind.

Many centuries later Mahatma Gandhi would take these teachings of the Gita as guiding principles for his life.

Gandhi saw the battlefield as a metaphor for our internal conflicts and Arjuna as the archetypal warrior within—one who sees through illusions to the truth and is able to act with courage and unwavering focus. Perhaps as a beginning yoga student, you have already encountered a glimpse of this warrior spirit in the standing pose Virabhadrasana II (or Vira II for short). In the deep lunge and open arms of this Warrior Pose variation, there is a challenging intensity—a marked contrast to the images of yoga as a passive practice intended for relaxation.

You may ask, “Why is there a Warrior Pose, when yoga is a practice of nonviolence?”

As a strong pose, Virabhadrasana II can teach modern yogis a lot about the dynamics of bringing wisdom into the actions of our everyday lives. It is a powerful pose, no doubt, but as you explore the pose’s alignment and inner attitude, the heart of the peaceful warrior begins to reveal itself.

As we go about our daily lives, we often speak of feeling “off-centered” or of needing to “get centered.” Being “centered” is a feeling of being balanced and at ease on all levels—physically, emotionally, mentally, spiritually.

It is the clear space of awareness from which the wise action within any moment can be found.

To find your center in Virabhadrasana II—the place where your energy is distributed evenly, without bias—start by grounding yourself within Tadasana (Mountain Pose). The training of a spiritual warrior begins here as you let go of any external distractions and bring your awareness to your core.

When you feel your mind settle within the relaxed steadiness of Tadasana, then prepare to begin Virabhadrasana II. Consciously step your feet apart in a wide stance (4 to 5 feet), with your heels aligned parallel to one another. Pivot your left foot out to a 90-degree angle and pivot your right foot in to a 45-degree angle. Plant your feet like roots, connecting through all of the corners of your feet while lifting through the arches.

To focus on the leg work of Virabhadrasana II first, keep your arms relaxed at your sides. Slowly bend your left knee until it comes directly over your ankle. Now explore the feeling of your weight. Is all of your weight in your front leg? Lunge deeper into your left leg and then shift your weight into your back leg to feel the extremes, and then try to find the balance point.

Let your left hip sink to draw you deeper into the lunge, but keep your right leg firm, inner thigh lifting, and outer edge of your right foot reaching into the ground. As your legs develop stamina and your hips open, you can begin to explore coming into a right angle with your front leg as if you are balancing something on your thigh.

Once you find the place where your weight is evenly distributed through your legs and hips, bring your awareness to your torso. Are you collapsing into your lower back? Find your vertical center by turning your tailbone toward the earth. Without tension, lift your lower belly, the seat of your power, towards the spine. This action will awaken your center, so you can begin to extend out of your lower back and spread your chest open. Now balance your rib cage directly over your pelvis. Is your torso turning towards the left leg? Draw your right side and the top of your right thigh back to feel yourself opening out from the center.

Keep scanning your body to feel where you are losing awareness and balance. Change sides and explore Virabhadrasana II to find that even flow of energy—north, south, east, and west—throughout the pose.

As a metaphor for living, yoga can help us see how unnecessary tension within our actions shifts us off-center. To maintain Warrior Pose, we often harden our eyes, hold our breath, or shrug our shoulders. Try Virabhadrasana II again on the left side, setting up your foundation from your center. Slowly raise your arms up to shoulder height, keeping your shoulder blades pressing into and down your back. Now, turn your gaze (drishti) to the middle finger of your left hand.

Like a Zen archer spotting a bull’s-eye, who practices just holding a bow for two years before ever releasing an arrow, find balance within your focus by becoming inwardly detached.

Let the backs of your eyes look inside while you stay totally present. Feel the power of your energy radiating freely from your center. Find the balance between working to your full potential and completely relaxing, mirroring the effortless stillness of an eagle hovering over a current of wind.

As you explore this dance between being active and receptive, you can contemplate Krishna’s paradoxical teaching, “One who can see action within inaction and inaction within action is the wisest among all beings.”

As you look out from this still point and open your inner ears, you may hear your internal warrior teacher giving you insights to bring you into balance not only in this moment, but in your life as a whole. Like Arjuna as Krishna whispered over his shoulder, you may be given confidence to face your fears, courage to move forward, compassion to embrace another, and wisdom to surrender to the one who holds the reins.

XRP Risks Losing $1 Amid Aggressive $2.5 Billion Shift

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

As XRP continues to struggle amid the broad crypto market volatility while failing in multiple attempts to recover, key on-chain metrics are pointing to increased selling pressure among traders.

While momentum remains extremely weak, latest data from crypto analytics platform, Cryptoquant, shows that sellers have dominated both XRP’s spot and futures markets, putting it at risk of losing the long-held $1 mark.

Per the data, XRP’s cumulative volume delta (CVD) across major cryptocurrency exchanges shows that aggressive selling has dominated both its spot and futures market since September 23.

As of November 12, XRP’s Perpetual CVD on Binance has fallen from -$109 million to about -$1.1 billion. It is important to note that the CVD of an asset is a key metric that measures the difference between the volume of aggressive buy orders and aggressive sell orders over time.

While the metric turns negative when more traders are aggressively selling than buying, the -991 million negative balance in the Binance perpetual CVD signals stronger selling pressure.

With the negative balance on Binance exceeding its previous level by more than 10 times, this shows that traders have placed significantly more market sell orders than buy orders, either by opening short positions or closing existing long positions.

Apart from its futures market, further data shows that sellers have also dominated XRP’s spot activity across all major crypto exchanges, including Binance.

Notably, XRP’s All CEX Estimated Spot CVD moved from -$400 million to -$2.9 billion over the same period.

While this marks a $2.5 billion increase in the imbalance favoring sellers, it indicates that XRP spot traders have also been selling XRP more aggressively rather than buying, propelling the asset for further price declines.

Player who left Sheffield Wednesday in the summer in line for new EFL contract

📅 2026-11-12  |  Read Source →

Sheffield Wednesday cut ties with the centre-back at the end of last season following their relegation from down to League One. SWFC decided not to hand him a fresh contract and he subsequently became a free agent in late June.

Iorfa, 31, weighed up his options over the summer as he searched for the right opportunity. He then eventually penned short-term terms with Rotherham United in September running until January after opting to stick around in South Yorkshire.

In this latest update regarding his long-term future with the Millers, McClaren has declared their intention to keep hold of him and has said, as per a report by Rotherham Advertiser: “He’s performing well for us and he’s a good leader. The lads like him, I like him. He’s got energy and personality. We’re conversing with him every day. We’d like to extend it. Nearer the time, I’m sure we’ll address it.”

Rotherham United went down to League Two earlier this year and have since struggled to adapt to life in the fourth tier. They are only above the drop zone on goal difference ahead of Colchester United.

Iorfa was a good servant for Sheffield Wednesday during his time with the Owls. He spent seven years at SWFC and made 222 appearances in all competitions, chipping in with seven goals from the back.

Despite Rotherham United’s struggles on the pitch over recent times, he has enjoyed plenty of game time with the Millers. He has made nine outings since his switch, eight of which have come in the league.

Iorfa was snapped up by Alex Bruce’s side to boost their defensive department. He gives them more competition and depth in his position.

The ex-England youth international spent the last three years in the Championship before his transfer to the New York Stadium. Therefore, the ex-Wolves man was seen as a bit of a coup for Rotherham United at the time.

Handing an extension to Iorfa is a no-brainer for Rotherham United. He is a vastly experienced operator in the EFL and will be a useful character to have in and around the dressing room.

The Millers need to rise away from the bottom two soon though. A club of their size should not be battling to avoid slipping into non-league.

Bruce was appointed as their head coach in the summer but he has won only two league games. He was previously Karl Robinson’s number two at Salford City and has also managed Macclesfield in the past.

Rotherham United won 2-0 away at Bromley in the FA Cup first round last time out. Next up is a trip to Blundell Park to take on Grimsby Town after the international break.

And the first of those matches will see Robertson win his 102nd cap for the Tartan Army, bringing him level with Dalglish at the top of the all-time charts.

Dalglish – nicknamed 'King Kenny' – represented Scotland from 1971 to 1986, scoring a joint-record 30 goals for his country (alongside Denis Law) and featuring at three World Cups.

Only Jim Leighton (nine) has appeared in more World Cup matches for Scotland than Dalglish, who is also one of just four players to net multiple goals at FIFA's flagship tournament for the Tartan Army (two, alongside John Wark, Archie Gemmill and Joe Jordan – the latter scored four goals).

And though Robertson will soon surpass Dalglish outright, he is in no doubt over the identity of Scotland's greatest-ever player.

"Friday night's not about me. Friday night is about the greatest player of all time in our country, and for me, that will always be Kenny.
